[EVENT: SYR115809]
Date: 2023-07-25
Country: Syria | Location: Sur
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: QSD: Syrian Democratic Forces [Rebel group]
Actor2: QSD: Syrian Democratic Forces [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 2
Sources: Alkhabour; SHAAM; SOHR; SY24; The Euphrates Post; Twitter [Other-New media]

DESCRIPTION:
On 25 July 2023, armed clashes took place between QSD-affiliated military police and MDA members at al Kua' checkpoint in Sur in Deir ez Zor province after members of the former attempted to detain MDA members, in conjunction with MDA members besieging the QSD-affiliated military police building and taking control of QSD-affiliated checkpoints in the community. Clashes also took place between MDA council members and supporting tribal militia against QSD-affiliated anti-terrorism unit members after the former attacked a QSD command center in the community, with MDA: Deir ez Zor military council members cutting off roads to prevent QSD reinforcements from reaching Sur and its vicinity and the QSD-affiliate anti-terrorism unit sending reinforcements to the community, resulting in the killing of two MDA Deir ez Zor military council members, the injury of several others, and QSD's detainment of several Ekeidat tribal militia members. On the same day, Global Coalition-affiliated warplanes and helicopters flew over the Sur area as a delegation travelled to the Sur subdistrict, where a meeting took place between a Global Coalition delegation and MDA members in which parties agreed to a ceasefire and to return to previous conditions in the community, with MDA and its supporting tribal militia withdrawing from Sur and releasing all QSD detainments made during clashes. 2 fatalities.

---

[EVENT: SYR115845]
Date: 2023-07-25
Country: Syria | Location: Babka
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Syria) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: SOHR [Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 25 July 2023, locals held a protest in Babka in Aleppo province denouncing HTS for conducting arbitrary arrests, intimidating people and violating homes.

[EVENT: SYR115676]
Date: 2023-07-25
Country: Syria | Location: Basira
Type: Battles > Non-state actor overtakes territory | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: QSD: Syrian Democratic Forces [Rebel group]
Actor2: QSD: Syrian Democratic Forces [Rebel group]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: SOHR; SY24; The Euphrates Post [Other-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 25 July 2023, Deir-ez-Zor Military Council supported by Deir-ez-Zor tribal militias clashed with QSD and took control of Basira town in Deir ez Zor province following clashes between QSD and Deir ez Zor military council members in Sur. Casualties unknown.

[EVENT: SYR115818]
Date: 2023-07-25
Country: Syria | Location: Mardak
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Unidentified Armed Group (Syria) [Political militia]
Actor2: Military Forces of Syria (2000-2024) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: SOHR; Suwayda24 [Other-Subnational]

DESCRIPTION:
On 25 July 2023, an unidentified armed group abducted a regime lieutenant colonel after intercepting him at the Mardak bridge along the Damascus-as Sweida road in as Sweida province. The incident took place after the family of an arrested young man released three regime members they had abducted in order to secure their son's release, who regime-affiliated state security members subsequently refused to release. Following the latest abduction, the regime sent reinforcements to the area, who fired live ammunition in the air upon their arrival. On 26 July, following mediation efforts, regime members released the arrested son and his family released the regime lieutenant they abducted.
